The marble walls frame polished granite tomb markings on all four walls; some which have fallen free and lie broken on the floor.   The remaining tombs display symbols drawn in blood. Beneath the symbol a single word is written with blood in common.  Upon closer inspection the word spells "eternal".

[Mausoleum]
Several benches crafted from stone rest against each wall. Candle holders,three mounted to each wall horizontally,display spent candles;the center holder,a red one accompanied by a black one on each side. A large circle drawn in the dusty floor displays an eye like symbol in its center.  Black and red candles recently burnt, alternate around the circles circumference; three red candles placed vertically make up the eyes center. Each of the cracked walls appears to have been broken simultaneously as evidenced by the nearly identical spider web pattern each wall displays. In the center of the western wall a single word written in what appears to be blood says "eminent"

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Sammoth the Rock Troll,  Clovis the Terrak,  Trin the Ghoblin
Obvious exits are: north, east, south

Like water from a fountain, the wisps spray up into the air before raining down upon Trin and then vanishing into air.

Wisps begin to dance up from the ground in front of Trin.

[Mausoleum]
The marble walls frame polished granite tomb markings on all four walls; some which have fallen free and lie broken on the floor.   The remaining tombs display symbols drawn in blood. Beneath the symbol a single word is written with blood in common.  Upon closer inspection the word spells "eternal".

[Crater Circle]
The white marble building to the north reflects those who pass it in its polished facade. A silver plaque reads "Council Building." Many are three to four times larger than life and can usually be found to have offerings placed beneath them. A large triple-wide staircase heads into the center of the crater to the south.

The area is illuminated.

[City Square Center]
Chattering in every language imaginable echoes through the area. This is considered the "Hub of the City." The center is dominated by a massive warhammer. It's head half embedded into the ground. Standing over it is a giant golden statue of a ghoblin. Small benches dot the area, and most of them are occupied at any given time of day or night. Stretched high above is a gossamer canopy flapping in the wind, offering a bit of protection from the weather.

The area is illuminated.

[City Center East]
Three small fountains dot the landscape. At one time adorable cherubs graced the top of the pink marble, but sometime in the past their faces were altered to be obviously Ghoblin. Silly grins, with water splashing across them, now stare out at those who walk through the area. Occasionally someone will throw a coin into the fountain and quietly make a wish. As soon as they leave the area though, a Ghoblin child will dash into the fountain and claim their prize.

The area is illuminated.

[Crater Circle]
Traffic clogs the simple streets. Carts attempt to negotiate the ramps to the northeast and east, and foot traffic looks to avoid the mess by descending into the bottom of the crater through the western stairs. A mule brays loudly trying to voice his displeasure at being coaxed down the road with a full cart. [Prophet Avenue]
A large wooden edifice leads to a large group of tunnels. Living in these tunnels are many of the areas Terrak's. The tunnels lead underground and in almost all cases the only thing visible are a few cooking and heating vents. It is not unusual to be watching a Terrak walking towards home and virtually disappearing from sight. Many wonder if they are seeing a trick of the light, or if they actually have the ability to disappear.

[East Street]
The blending of two different societies come together at this intersection. Terrak homes dot the northeastern corner and multi-colored Ghoblin homes on the southeastern corner. All of these lie in the shadow of the garishly large golden building that rises to the west. Its over bearing size blocks out the light to the area during most of the daytime hours, barely allowing the light of dusk to permeate the sky in the late evenings.

[East Street]
Oddly sized buildings throw mysterious shadows across the street creating an odd pattern of light and dark. Many have difficulties traversing this road as they never quite are able to adjust their sight from light to dark and back to light again. Between buildings dark and hooded visages blend into the framework. It is often not obvious that there actually was a person there, or just the buildings playing tricks with their shadows.

[Northwest Avenue]
A large granite marker sits in the middle of the street, with the side facing the northwest welcoming people to the Ghoblin District. As well as welcoming visitors, it reminds them to mind their coin purse and prohibits non-licensed Mynarians from entering the area. An opulent gilded storefront lines the western side of the street, dried and fresh bats hanging from their feet in the windows.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: southeast, northwest, door

nw

[Northwest Avenue]
The intersection of Northwest Avenue and Balance Street brings together the different manner in which the original buildings of Geddon have been refurbished. To the southeast the opulent buildings of the Ghoblin disctrict can be seen, while to the northwest the more refined marble of the Garudan district. To the west a mixture of wooden and granite residences stretch down Balance Street.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: southeast, west, northwest

nw

[Northwest Avenue]
One of the few remaining original buildings left in this district, the library of the Geddon Sage guild, rises high into the sky. Constructed centuries ago by the Mynarians, the massive granite blocks would prove difficult for even a team of terraks to move. Being a secretive group, the sage guild prohibits entrance to the library to most everyone, though their supply store is open to the public.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: southeast, northwest, door

nw

[Citadel Way]
A watchtower in the wall rises high above the intersection of Citadel Way and Northeast Ave.  Guards look down from high above, both into the city and outside the city walls. An ancient Mynarian statue, carved from raw granite, rests along the southwest corner of the intersection. Too heavy to move the statue sits headless, as long ago defaced it by cutting off the statue's head.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: southeast, west, door

Backpacks, satchels, and all manner of containers, as well as provisions can be found on neatly kept shelves. The proprietor putters around straightening up his merchandise and keeping an eye on those who enter.

[Citadel Way]
Rising high above the street and directly in its center is a large granite podium. Sitting at the top of the podium, which was built out of old Mynarian buildings, is a priestess of Aridal in meditation. Reconstruction is underway along the north city wall, with cracked sections being shored up as well as polished marble being fitted over the original granite.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: east, west, door

go door

[Alchemy Shop]
The interior of Lorhael's Alchemy shop is near immaculate. Marble counter tops, white as fresh fallen snow, are polished to the point of a mirror-like finish. Crystal cauldrons of all shapes and sizes sit on lacquered oak shelves.  In the glass fronted counter vials containing liquids and powders of every color and consistency imaginable are displayed for sale. From the back of the store wisps of vapors and fumes drift slowly out, twirling seemingly on their own.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: door
